=== Performance Notes ===
Kademlia advantages:
  - O(log n) hops for lookups
  - Parallel queries (alpha=3) speed up lookups
  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing
  - Flexible routing (multiple paths to destination)
  - Better resilience to node failures

  - Parallel queries (alpha=3) speed up lookups
  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing
  - Flexible routing (multiple paths to destination)
  - Better resilience to node failures

=== Performance Notes ===
Kademlia advantages:
  - O(log n) hops for lookups
  - Parallel queries (alpha=3) speed up lookups
  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing
  - Flexible routing (multiple paths to destination)
  - Better resilience to node failures

=== Performance Notes ===
Kademlia advantages:
  - O(log n) hops for lookups
  - Parallel queries (alpha=3) speed up lookups
  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing
  - Flexible routing (multiple paths to destination)
  - Better resilience to node failures

Kademlia advantages:

  - Parallel queries (alpha=3) speed up lookups
  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing
  - Flexible routing (multiple paths to destination)
  - Better resilience to node failures

=== Performance Notes ===
Kademlia advantages:
  - O(log n) hops for lookups
  - Parallel queries (alpha=3) speed up lookups
  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing
  - Flexible routing (multiple paths to destination)
  - Better resilience to node failures

  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing

=== Performance Notes ===
Kademlia advantages:
  - O(log n) hops for lookups
  - Parallel queries (alpha=3) speed up lookups
  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing
  - Flexible routing (multiple paths to destination)
  - Better resilience to node failures

=== Performance Notes ===
Kademlia advantages:
  - O(log n) hops for lookups
  - Parallel queries (alpha=3) speed up lookups
  - XOR metric provides symmetric routing
  - Flexible routing (multiple paths to destination)
  - Better resilience to node failures
